```
            return;

        }
    }
}
```

```
public class DivArrayByTwo {
    //@  requires arr == null;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@ also
    //@  requires arr != null;
    //@  assigns arr[*];
    //@  ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] == \old(arr[i]) / 2;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static void DivArrayByTwo(int[] arr) {
        if (arr == null)
            return;

        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; arr[j] == \old(arr[j]) / 2;
        //@ maintaining \forall int k; i <= k < arr.length; arr[k] == \old(arr[k]);
        //@ loop_writes arr[*], i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; ++i) {
            arr[i] = arr[i] / 2;
        }
    }
}
```

```
public class IsPrefix {
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}

```
Note that:
+ You SHOULD generate `ensures` statements for every method, and `maintaining` statements for every loop.

+ You SHOULD output the code in its entirety, withou omitting any original content.

assistant: ```
public class IsPrefix {
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() <= txt.length();
    //@  ensures \result == (\forall int i; 0 <= i < pat.length(); pat.charAt(i) == txt.charAt(i));
    //@ also
    //@  requires pat != null;
    //@  requires txt != null;
    //@  requires pat.length() > txt.length();
    //@  ensures \result == false;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    public static boolean isPrefix(String pat, String txt) {
        if (pat.length() > txt.length())
            return false;

        int i = 0;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= pat.length();
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; pat.charAt(j) == txt.charAt(j);
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases pat.length() - i;
        while (i < pat.length()) {
            if (pat.charAt(i) != txt.charAt(i))
                return false;
            i = i + 1;
        }

        return true;
    }
}
```

```
public class Find {
    public static int find(int[] arr, int elem) {
        if (arr == null)
            return -1;

        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; i++)
            if (arr[i] == elem)
                return i;
        return -1;
    }
}

```

assistant: ```
public class Find {
    //@  requires arr != null;
    //@  requires \exists int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] == elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures 0 <= \result < arr.length;
    //@  ensures \forall int i; 0 <= i < \result; arr[i] != elem;
    //@  ensures arr[\result] == elem;
    //@ also
    //@  requires arr == null || \forall int i; 0 <= i < arr.length; arr[i] != elem;
    //@  assigns \nothing;
    //@  ensures \result == -1;
    //@ behaviors disjoint;
    //@ behaviors complete;
    //@ pure
    public static int find(int[] arr, int elem) {
        if (arr == null)
            return -1;
        //@ maintaining 0 <= i <= arr.length;
        //@ maintaining \forall int j; 0 <= j < i; arr[j] != elem;
        //@ loop_writes i;
        //@ decreases arr.length - i;
        for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; i++)
            if (arr[i] == elem)
                return i;
        return -1;
    }
}
```

```
    public static void CopyArray(int[] b, int iBegin, int iEnd, int[] a) {
        int k = iBegin;
```

```
        //@ maintaining iBegin <= k && k <= iEnd;
```
```java
        //@ maintaining (\forall int m; iBegin <= m && m < k; a[m] == b[m]);
```

    //@ ensures (\forall int k; iBegin <= k && k < iEnd; a[k] == b[k]);
    //@ ensures (\forall int k; 0 <= k && k < iBegin || k >= iEnd && k <= a.length; a[k] == \old(a[k]));  
    public static void CopyArray(int[] b, int iBegin, int iEnd, int[] a) {
        int k = iBegin;

        //@ maintaining iBegin <= k && k <= iEnd;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int m; iBegin <= m && m < k; a[m] == b[m]);
        while (iEnd - k > 0) {
            a[k] = b[k];
            k = k + 1;
        }
    }
}

    //@ requires a != null;
    //@ requires 0 <= iBegin && iBegin <= iEnd && iEnd <= b.length && iEnd <= a.length;
    //@ assigns a[iBegin..iEnd-1];
    //@ ensures (\forall int k; iBegin <= k && k < iEnd; a[k] == b[k]);
    //@ ensures (\forall int k; 0 - 1 <= k && k <= iBegin || k > iEnd && k <= a.length; a[k] == \old(a[k]));  
    public static void CopyArray(int[] b, int iBegin, int iEnd, int[] a) {
        int k = iBegin;

        //@ maintaining iBegin <= k && k <= iEnd;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int m; iBegin <= m && m < k; a[m] == b[m]);
        while (iEnd - k > 0) {
            a[k] = b[k];
            k = k + 1;
        }
    }
}

public class CopyArray {
    //@ requires b != null;
    //@ requires a != null;
    //@ requires 0 <= iBegin && iBegin <= iEnd && iEnd <= b.length && iEnd <= a.length;
    //@ assigns a[iBegin..iEnd-1];
    //@ ensures (\forall int k; iBegin <= k && k < iEnd; a[k] == b[k]);
    //@ ensures (\exists int k; 0 < k && k < iBegin && k + 1 >= iEnd || k <= a.length; a[k] == \old(a[k]));  
    public static void CopyArray(int[] b, int iBegin, int iEnd, int[] a) {
        int k = iBegin;

        //@ maintaining iBegin <= k && k <= iEnd;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int m; iBegin <= m && m < k; a[m] == b[m]);
        while (iEnd - k > 0) {
            a[k] = b[k];
            k = k + 1;
        }
    }
}

    //@ requires b != null;
    //@ requires a != null;
    //@ requires 0 <= iBegin && iBegin <= iEnd && iEnd <= b.length && iEnd <= a.length;
    //@ assigns a[iBegin..iEnd-1];
    //@ ensures (\forall int k; iBegin <= k && k < iEnd; a[k] == b[k]);
    //@ ensures (\exists int k; 0 < k && k <= iBegin && k > iEnd || k <= a.length; a[k] == \old(a[k]));  
    public static void CopyArray(int[] b, int iBegin, int iEnd, int[] a) {
        int k = iBegin;

        //@ maintaining iBegin <= k && k <= iEnd;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int m; iBegin <= m && m < k; a[m] == b[m]);
        while (iEnd - k > 0) {
            a[k] = b[k];
            k = k + 1;
        }
    }
}

    //@ requires a != null;
    //@ requires 0 <= iBegin && iBegin <= iEnd && iEnd <= b.length && iEnd <= a.length;
    //@ assigns a[iBegin..iEnd-1];
    //@ ensures (\forall int k; iBegin <= k && k < iEnd; a[k] == b[k]);
    //@ ensures (\exists int k; 0 < k && k <= iBegin || k >= iEnd || k <= a.length; a[k] == \old(a[k]));  
    public static void CopyArray(int[] b, int iBegin, int iEnd, int[] a) {
        int k = iBegin;

        //@ maintaining iBegin <= k && k <= iEnd;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int m; iBegin <= m && m < k; a[m] == b[m]);
        while (iEnd - k > 0) {
            a[k] = b[k];
            k = k + 1;
        }
    }
}

    //@ requires a != null;
    //@ requires 0 <= iBegin && iBegin <= iEnd && iEnd <= b.length && iEnd <= a.length;
    //@ assigns a[iBegin..iEnd-1];
    //@ ensures (\forall int k; iBegin <= k && k < iEnd; a[k] == b[k]);
    //@ ensures (\exists int k; 0 <= k && k < iBegin && k + 1 >= iEnd || k <= a.length; a[k] == \old(a[k]));  
    public static void CopyArray(int[] b, int iBegin, int iEnd, int[] a) {
        int k = iBegin;

        //@ maintaining iBegin <= k && k <= iEnd;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int m; iBegin <= m && m < k; a[m] == b[m]);
        while (iEnd - k > 0) {
            a[k] = b[k];
            k = k + 1;
        }
    }
}

    //@ requires a != null;
    //@ requires 0 <= iBegin && iBegin <= iEnd && iEnd <= b.length && iEnd <= a.length;
    //@ assigns a[iBegin..iEnd-1];
    //@ ensures (\forall int k; iBegin <= k && k < iEnd; a[k] == b[k]);
    //@ ensures (\exists int k; 0 <= k && k <= iBegin && k > iEnd || k <= a.length; a[k] == \old(a[k]));  
    public static void CopyArray(int[] b, int iBegin, int iEnd, int[] a) {
        int k = iBegin;

        //@ maintaining iBegin <= k && k <= iEnd;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int m; iBegin <= m && m < k; a[m] == b[m]);
        while (iEnd - k > 0) {
            a[k] = b[k];
            k = k + 1;
        }
    }
}

    //@ requires 0 <= iBegin && iBegin <= iEnd && iEnd <= b.length && iEnd <= a.length;
    //@ assigns a[iBegin..iEnd-1];
    //@ ensures (\forall int k; iBegin <= k && k < iEnd; a[k] == b[k]);
    //@ ensures (\exists int k; 0 <= k && k <= iBegin || k >= iEnd || k <= a.length; a[k] == \old(a[k]));  
    public static void CopyArray(int[] b, int iBegin, int iEnd, int[] a) {
        int k = iBegin;

        //@ maintaining iBegin <= k && k <= iEnd;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int m; iBegin <= m && m < k; a[m] == b[m]);
        while (iEnd - k > 0) {
            a[k] = b[k];
            k = k + 1;
        }
    }
}

--------------- Mutation Iteration 144 ---------------
public class CopyArray {
    //@ requires b != null;
    //@ requires a != null;
    //@ requires 0 <= iBegin && iBegin <= iEnd && iEnd <= b.length && iEnd <= a.length;
    //@ assigns a[iBegin..iEnd-1];
    //@ ensures (\forall int k; iBegin <= k && k < iEnd; a[k] == b[k]);
    //@ ensures (\exists int k; 0 - 1 <= k && k < iBegin && k + 1 >= iEnd || k <= a.length; a[k] == \old(a[k]));  
    public static void CopyArray(int[] b, int iBegin, int iEnd, int[] a) {
        int k = iBegin;

        //@ maintaining iBegin <= k && k <= iEnd;
        //@ maintaining (\forall int m; iBegin <= m && m < k; a[m] == b[m]);
        while (iEnd - k > 0) {
            a[k] = b[k];
            k = k + 1;
        }
    }
}
